## M-PK-A7 A.7 Match numbers to groups

### Level 1

**Seed 1** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each number to its group.
- Answer: 2 ↔ 2 🦆, 3 ↔ 3 🦆, 5 ↔ 5 🦆
- Explanation, spoken: Count each group. 2 goes with 2 ducks. 3 goes with 3 ducks. 5 goes with 5 ducks.
- Explanation, shown: Count each group. / 2 goes with 2 ducks. / 3 goes with 3 ducks. / 5 goes with 5 ducks.
- Explanation picture: 2 and 2 🦆

**Seed 2** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each number to its group.
- Answer: 1 ↔ 1 🐝, 3 ↔ 3 🐝, 5 ↔ 5 🐝
- Explanation, spoken: Count each group. 1 goes with 1 bee. 3 goes with 3 bees. 5 goes with 5 bees.
- Explanation, shown: Count each group. / 1 goes with 1 bee. / 3 goes with 3 bees. / 5 goes with 5 bees.
- Explanation picture: 1 and 1 🐝

**Seed 3** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each number to its group.
- Answer: 2 ↔ 2 🍪, 3 ↔ 3 🍪, 5 ↔ 5 🍪
- Explanation, spoken: Count each group. 2 goes with 2 cookies. 3 goes with 3 cookies. 5 goes with 5 cookies.
- Explanation, shown: Count each group. / 2 goes with 2 cookies. / 3 goes with 3 cookies. / 5 goes with 5 cookies.
- Explanation picture: 2 and 2 🍪

### Level 2

**Seed 1** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each number to its group.
- Answer: 4 ↔ 4 🍪, 8 ↔ 8 🍪, in rows of five, 9 ↔ 9 🍪, in rows of five
- Explanation, spoken: Count each group. 4 goes with 4 cookies. 8 goes with 8 cookies. 9 goes with 9 cookies.
- Explanation, shown: Count each group. / 4 goes with 4 cookies. / 8 goes with 8 cookies. / 9 goes with 9 cookies.
- Explanation picture: 4 and 4 🍪

**Seed 2** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each number to its group.
- Answer: 1 ↔ 1 🚗, 5 ↔ 5 🚗, 10 ↔ 10 🚗, in rows of five
- Explanation, spoken: Count each group. 1 goes with 1 car. 5 goes with 5 cars. 10 goes with 10 cars.
- Explanation, shown: Count each group. / 1 goes with 1 car. / 5 goes with 5 cars. / 10 goes with 10 cars.
- Explanation picture: 1 and 1 🚗

**Seed 3** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each number to its group.
- Answer: 6 ↔ 6 ⭐, in rows of five, 7 ↔ 7 ⭐, in rows of five, 10 ↔ 10 ⭐, in rows of five
- Explanation, spoken: Count each group. 6 goes with 6 stars. 7 goes with 7 stars. 10 goes with 10 stars.
- Explanation, shown: Count each group. / 6 goes with 6 stars. / 7 goes with 7 stars. / 10 goes with 10 stars.
- Explanation picture: 6 and 6 ⭐, in rows of five

### Lesson

- see: A number tells how many are in a group. (picture: 3 and 3 🍎)
- watch: Count each group. 2 goes with 2 apples. 4 goes with 4 apples. (picture: 4 and 4 🍎)
- try: Which number goes with these stars? (picture: 5 ⭐)
  - then: 5 goes with 5 stars.
- remember: Count the group, then find its number. (picture: 2 and 2 🍎)

## M-PK-B1 B.1 Which has more

### Lesson

- remember: Listen for joining or taking away. (picture: 5 🍪, 2 crossed out)

- Picture: 6 + 5 = □
- Answer: type 11
- Explanation, spoken: Start at 6. Count on 5 more. 11.
- Explanation, shown: Start at 6. / Count on 5 more: 7, 8, 9, 10, 11. / 6 + 5 = 11
- Explanation picture: 6 + 5 = 11

### Lesson

- see: To add, start with the bigger number and count on. (picture: number line from 0 to 20 by 1, dot at 8)
- watch: 8 plus 3. Start at 8. Count on 3 more: 9, 10, 11. (picture: 8 + 3 = 11)
- try: 4 plus 9 equals what? (picture: 4 + 9 = □)

- remember: Start with the bigger number. (picture: number line from 0 to 20 by 1)

## M-1-B6 B.6 True or false equations

### Level 1

- Answer: False
- Explanation, spoken: The left side is 6. The right side is 8. They are not the same.
- Explanation, shown: The left side is 6. / The right side is 8. / They are not the same.
- Explanation picture: 1 + 5 ≠ 8

**Seed 2** (choice, look)

- On screen: Is this true?

- Options: True | **False**
- Answer: False
- Explanation, spoken: The left side is 5. The right side is 6. They are not the same.

- On screen: Is this true?

- Options: True | **False**
- Answer: False
- Explanation, spoken: The left side is 6. The right side is 4. They are not the same.
- Explanation, shown: The left side is 6. / The right side is 4. / They are not the same.

- Options: True | **False**
- Answer: False
- Explanation, spoken: The left side is 6. The right side is 8. They are not the same.
- Explanation, shown: The left side is 6. / The right side is 8. / They are not the same.
- Explanation picture: 3 + 3 ≠ 5 + 3

- On screen: Is this true?
- Picture: 1 + 4 = 3 + 4
- Options: True | **False**
- Answer: False
- Explanation, spoken: The left side is 5. The right side is 7. They are not the same.
- Explanation, shown: The left side is 5. / The right side is 7. / They are not the same.

- Explanation, shown: The left side is 3. / The right side is 1. / They are not the same.

### Lesson

- see: The equals sign means both sides are the same amount. (picture: 4 + 1 = 5)

- remember: Both sides must be the same amount. (picture: 3 + 2 = 2 + 3)

- Answer: 38

- Options: **72** | 73 | 82
- Answer: 72
- Explanation, spoken: Add the ones: 9 plus 3 is 12. That is one ten and 2 ones. Add the extra ten to the tens. 72.
- Explanation, shown: Add the ones: 9 plus 3 is 12. / That is 1 ten and 2 ones. / Add the extra ten to the tens: 72.
- Explanation picture: 69 + 3 = 72

### Lesson

- see: To add to a two-digit number, add the ones first. (picture: 2 tens, 3 ones)
- watch: 23 plus 4. Add the ones: 3 plus 4 is 7. The tens stay the same. The answer is 27. (picture: 23 + 4 = 27)
- try: 36 plus 7 equals what? (picture: 36 + 7 = □)
  - then: Add the ones: 6 plus 7 is 13. That is 1 ten and 3 ones. Add the extra ten to the tens: 43.
- remember: Ones with ones, tens with tens. (picture: 23 + 4 = 27)

- Explanation picture: 9 + 5 = 14

- Answer: 12

- Explanation, shown: 4 plus 8 is 12.
- Explanation picture: 4 + 8 = 12

### Level 2

- On screen: There are 17 cookies. Mia takes 7. How many are left?
- Options: 7 | 9 | **10**
- Answer: 10
- Explanation, spoken: 17 minus 7 is 10.
- Explanation, shown: 17 minus 7 is 10.
- Explanation picture: 17 - 7 = 10

**Seed 2** (choice, look)

- On screen: There are 12 flowers. Sam takes 9. How many are left?
- Options: **3** | 9 | 12
- Answer: 3
- Explanation, spoken: 12 minus 9 is 3.
- Explanation, shown: 12 minus 9 is 3.
- Explanation picture: 12 - 9 = 3

**Seed 3** (choice, look)

- On screen: There are 10 flowers. Mia takes 3. How many are left?
- Options: 3 | **7** | 10
- Answer: 7
- Explanation, spoken: 10 minus 3 is 7.
- Explanation, shown: 10 minus 3 is 7.
- Explanation picture: 10 - 3 = 7

### Level 3

**Seed 1** (numberPad, look)

- On screen: Max has 15 balloons. Mia has 10. How many more does Max have?
- Answer: type 5
- Explanation, spoken: 15 minus 10 is 5.
- Explanation, shown: 15 minus 10 is 5.
- Explanation picture: 15 - 10 = 5

**Seed 2** (numberPad, look)

- On screen: Sam has 14 balls. Leo has 5. How many more does Sam have?
- Answer: type 9
- Explanation, spoken: 14 minus 5 is 9.
- Explanation, shown: 14 minus 5 is 9.

**Seed 3** (numberPad, look)

- Explanation, shown: 14 minus 3 is 11.
- Explanation picture: 14 - 3 = 11

### Lesson

- see: A story tells us what to do. Getting more means plus. Giving away means minus. (picture: 8 + 5)
- watch: Sam has 8 apples. Sam gets 5 more. How many apples does Sam have now? 8 plus 5 is 13. (picture: 8 + 5 = 13)
- try: Mia has 12 apples. Mia gives away 4. How many apples are left? (picture: 12 - 4 = □)
  - then: 12 minus 4 is 8.
- remember: Listen for joining or taking away. (picture: 12 - 4 = 8)

## M-2-A3 A.3 Compare three-digit numbers

### Level 1

**Seed 1** (choice, look)

- On screen: Which number is less?
- Options: 664 | **102**
- Answer: 102
- Explanation, spoken: Look at the biggest place first. 664 has more hundreds. 102 is less than 664.
- Explanation, shown: Look at the biggest place first. / 664 has more hundreds. / 102 is less than 664.
- Explanation picture: 664 > 102

- Answer: 760
- Explanation, spoken: Look at the biggest place first. 760 has more hundreds. 760 is greater than 392.
- Explanation, shown: Look at the biggest place first. / 760 has more hundreds. / 760 is greater than 392.
- Explanation picture: 760 > 392

- Explanation picture: 748 > 134

- Explanation picture: 664 > 600

**Seed 2** (choice, look)

- On screen: Which number is greater?
- Options: **760** | 732
- Answer: 760
- Explanation, spoken: The hundreds are the same. Look at the tens. 760 has more tens. 760 is greater than 732.
- Explanation, shown: The hundreds are the same. Look at the tens. / 760 has more tens. / 760 is greater than 732.
- Explanation picture: 760 > 732

**Seed 3** (choice, look)

- On screen: Which number is greater?
- Options: **748** | 703
- Answer: 748
- Explanation, spoken: The hundreds are the same. Look at the tens. 748 has more tens. 748 is greater than 703.
- Explanation, shown: The hundreds are the same. Look at the tens. / 748 has more tens. / 748 is greater than 703.
- Explanation picture: 748 > 703

### Level 3

**Seed 1** (choice, look)

- On screen: Which sign goes between 102 and 574?
- Picture: 102 □ 574
- Options: > | **<** | =
- Answer: <
- Explanation, spoken: Look at the biggest place first. 574 has more hundreds. 574 is greater than 102.
- Explanation, shown: Look at the biggest place first. / 574 has more hundreds. / 574 is greater than 102.
- Explanation picture: 102 < 574

**Seed 2** (choice, look)

- On screen: Which sign goes between 584 and 887?
- Picture: 584 □ 887
- Options: > | **<** | =
- Answer: <
- Explanation, spoken: Look at the biggest place first. 887 has more hundreds. 887 is greater than 584.
- Explanation, shown: Look at the biggest place first. / 887 has more hundreds. / 887 is greater than 584.
- Explanation picture: 584 < 887

**Seed 3** (choice, look)

- Picture: 134 □ 134
- Options: > | < | **=**
- Answer: =
- Explanation, spoken: 134 and 134 are the same number. They are equal.
- Explanation, shown: 134 and 134 are the same number. / They are equal.
- Explanation picture: 134 = 134

### Lesson

- see: To compare big numbers, start with the hundreds. (picture: 524 and 389)
- watch: Look at the biggest place first. 524 has more hundreds. 524 is greater than 389. (picture: 524 > 389)
- try: Which number is greater? (picture: 641 and 672)
  - then: The hundreds are the same. Look at the tens. 672 has more tens. 672 is greater than 641.
- remember: Compare the biggest place first. (picture: 524 > 389)

- try: Mia has 48 apples. Mia gives away 12. How many apples are left? (picture: 48 - 12 = □)

- Explanation picture: 21 + 2 = 23 and 23 - 12 = 11

**Seed 2** (numberPad, look)

- On screen: Ava has 24 bananas. Ava gets 8 more. Then Ava gives away 7. How many bananas does Ava have now?
- Answer: type 25
- Explanation, spoken: First step: 24 plus 8 is 32. Second step: 32 minus 7 is 25.
- Explanation, shown: First step: 24 plus 8 is 32. / Second step: 32 minus 7 is 25.
- Explanation picture: 24 + 8 = 32 and 32 - 7 = 25

**Seed 3** (numberPad, look)

- On screen: Sam has 23 strawberries. Sam gets 2 more. Then Sam gives away 10. How many strawberries does Sam have now?
- Answer: type 15
- Explanation, spoken: First step: 23 plus 2 is 25. Second step: 25 minus 10 is 15.
- Explanation, shown: First step: 23 plus 2 is 25. / Second step: 25 minus 10 is 15.
- Explanation picture: 23 + 2 = 25 and 25 - 10 = 15

### Lesson

- see: Some stories have two steps. Do one step at a time. (picture: 6 + 4 + 3)
- watch: Leo has 6 apples. Leo gets 4 more. Then Leo gets 3 more. How many apples does Leo have now? First step: 6 plus 4 is 10. Second step: 10 plus 3 is 13. (picture: 6 + 4 = 10 and 10 + 3 = 13)
- try: Ava has 8 apples. Ava gets 5 more. Then Ava gives away 2. How many apples does Ava have now? (picture: 8 + 5 - 2 = □)
  - then: First step: 8 plus 5 is 13. Second step: 13 minus 2 is 11.
- remember: One step, then the next. (picture: 6 + 4 = 10 and 10 + 3 = 13)

## E-PK-A3 A.3 Match uppercase and lowercase

### Level 1

**Seed 1** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each big letter to its small letter.
- Answer: Q ↔ q, R ↔ r, E ↔ e
- Explanation, spoken: Big Q goes with small q. Big R goes with small r. Big E goes with small e.
- Explanation, shown: Big Q goes with small q. / Big R goes with small r. / Big E goes with small e.
- Explanation picture: Q and q

**Seed 2** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each big letter to its small letter.
- Answer: A ↔ a, T ↔ t, G ↔ g
- Explanation, spoken: Big A goes with small a. Big T goes with small t. Big G goes with small g.
- Explanation, shown: Big A goes with small a. / Big T goes with small t. / Big G goes with small g.
- Explanation picture: A and a

**Seed 3** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each big letter to its small letter.
- Answer: T ↔ t, H ↔ h, B ↔ b
- Explanation, spoken: Big T goes with small t. Big H goes with small h. Big B goes with small b.
- Explanation, shown: Big T goes with small t. / Big H goes with small h. / Big B goes with small b.
- Explanation picture: T and t

### Level 2

**Seed 1** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each big letter to its small letter.
- Answer: V ↔ v, H ↔ h, U ↔ u
- Explanation, spoken: Big V goes with small v. Big H goes with small h. Big U goes with small u.
- Explanation, shown: Big V goes with small v. / Big H goes with small h. / Big U goes with small u.
- Explanation picture: V and v

**Seed 2** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each big letter to its small letter.
- Answer: V ↔ v, B ↔ b, U ↔ u
- Explanation, spoken: Big V goes with small v. Big B goes with small b. Big U goes with small u.
- Explanation, shown: Big V goes with small v. / Big B goes with small b. / Big U goes with small u.
- Explanation picture: V and v

**Seed 3** (match, look)

- On screen: Match each big letter to its small letter.
- Answer: U ↔ u, V ↔ v, G ↔ g
- Explanation, spoken: Big U goes with small u. Big V goes with small v. Big G goes with small g.
- Explanation, shown: Big U goes with small u. / Big V goes with small v. / Big G goes with small g.
- Explanation picture: U and u

### Lesson

- see: Every letter has a big shape and a small shape. Big A and small a. (picture: A and a)
- watch: Big B goes with small b. (picture: B and b)
- try: Which small letter goes with big G? (picture: G and t and g)
  - then: Big G goes with small g.
- remember: Every letter has a big shape and a small shape. (picture: R and r)

## E-PK-A5 A.5 Letter or number

### Level 1

**Seed 1** (sort, look)

- On screen: Put each one in its group.
- Bins: Letters | Numbers
- Answer: t → Letters, 4 → Numbers, M → Letters, 9 → Numbers
- Explanation, spoken: The letters are t M. The numbers are 4 9.
- Explanation, shown: Letters: t, M. / Numbers: 4, 9.
- Explanation picture: t and M

**Seed 2** (sort, look)

- On screen: Put each one in its group.
- Bins: Letters | Numbers
- Answer: 6 → Numbers, W → Letters, X → Letters, n → Letters
- Explanation, spoken: The letters are W X n. The number is 6.
- Explanation, shown: Letters: W, X, n. / Number: 6.
- Explanation picture: W and X and n

**Seed 3** (sort, look)

- On screen: Put each one in its group.
- Bins: Letters | Numbers
- Answer: K → Letters, V → Letters, 4 → Numbers, m → Letters
- Explanation, spoken: The letters are K V m. The number is 4.
- Explanation, shown: Letters: K, V, m. / Number: 4.
- Explanation picture: K and V and m

### Level 2

**Seed 1** (sort, look)

- On screen: Put each one in its group.
- Bins: Letters | Numbers
- Answer: y → Letters, 7 → Numbers, 4 → Numbers, t → Letters, M → Letters, 9 → Numbers
- Explanation, spoken: The letters are y t M. The numbers are 7 4 9.
- Explanation, shown: Letters: y, t, M. / Numbers: 7, 4, 9.
- Explanation picture: y and t and M

**Seed 2** (sort, look)

- On screen: Put each one in its group.
- Bins: Letters | Numbers
- Answer: 9 → Numbers, W → Letters, n → Letters, m → Letters, X → Letters, 6 → Numbers
- Explanation, spoken: The letters are W n m X. The numbers are 9 6.
- Explanation, shown: Letters: W, n, m, X. / Numbers: 9, 6.
- Explanation picture: W and n and m and X

**Seed 3** (sort, look)

- On screen: Put each one in its group.
- Bins: Letters | Numbers
- Answer: m → Letters, V → Letters, 7 → Numbers, K → Letters, 4 → Numbers, r → Letters
- Explanation, spoken: The letters are m V K r. The numbers are 7 4.
- Explanation, shown: Letters: m, V, K, r. / Numbers: 7, 4.
- Explanation picture: m and V and K and r

### Lesson

- see: Letters make words. Numbers tell how many. (picture: m and 4)
- watch: R is a letter. 7 is a number. (picture: R and 7)
- try: Is this a letter or a number? (picture: k)
  - then: k is a letter.
- remember: Things in a group share something. (picture: a and t and m)

## E-PK-B3 B.3 Count syllables

### Level 1

- On screen: How many claps in apple?
- Picture: apple
- Options: 1 | **2**
- Answer: 2
- Explanation, spoken: apple. 2 claps.
- Explanation, shown: ap · ple / 2 claps.
- Explanation picture: ap · ple

**Seed 2** (choice, look)

- On screen: How many claps in turtle?
- Picture: turtle
- Options: 1 | **2**
- Answer: 2
- Explanation, spoken: turtle. 2 claps.
- Explanation, shown: tur · tle / 2 claps.
- Explanation picture: tur · tle

**Seed 3** (choice, look)

- On screen: How many claps in apple?
- Picture: apple
- Options: 1 | **2**
- Answer: 2
- Explanation, spoken: apple. 2 claps.
- Explanation, shown: ap · ple / 2 claps.
- Explanation picture: ap · ple

### Level 2

**Seed 1** (choice, look)

- On screen: How many claps in apple?
- Picture: apple
- Options: 1 | **2** | 3
- Answer: 2
- Explanation, spoken: apple. 2 claps.
- Explanation, shown: ap · ple / 2 claps.
- Explanation picture: ap · ple

**Seed 2** (choice, look)

- On screen: How many claps in umbrella?
- Picture: umbrella
- Options: 1 | 2 | **3**
- Answer: 3
- Explanation, spoken: umbrella. 3 claps.
- Explanation, shown: um · brel · la / 3 claps.
- Explanation picture: um · brel · la

**Seed 3** (choice, look)

- On screen: How many claps in banana?
- Picture: banana
- Options: 1 | 2 | **3**
- Answer: 3
- Explanation, spoken: banana. 3 claps.
- Explanation, shown: ba · na · na / 3 claps.
- Explanation picture: ba · na · na

### Lesson

- see: Words have beats. Clap once for each beat. (picture: cat)
- watch: How many claps in apple? ap · ple. 2 claps. (picture: ap · ple)
- try: How many claps in rabbit? (picture: rabbit)
  - then: rab · bit. 2 claps.
- remember: Each clap has one vowel sound. (picture: ap · ple)

- Picture: purple square, red square, purple triangle, red triangle, purple square, red square, purple triangle, blank
- Options: purple triangle | **red triangle** | red square
- Answer: red triangle
- Explanation, spoken: The shape goes square, square, triangle, triangle. The colour goes purple, red. Next is a red triangle.
- Explanation, shown: The shape goes square, square, triangle, triangle. / The colour goes purple, red. / Next is a red triangle.
- Explanation picture: red triangle

**Seed 3** (choice, look)

- On screen: What comes next?
- Picture: yellow heart, yellow square, purple heart, purple square, yellow heart, yellow square, blank
- Options: **purple heart** | yellow heart | purple square
- Answer: purple heart
- Explanation, spoken: The colour goes yellow, yellow, purple, purple. The shape goes heart, square. Next is a purple heart.
- Explanation, shown: The colour goes yellow, yellow, purple, purple. / The shape goes heart, square. / Next is a purple heart.
- Explanation picture: purple heart

### Lesson

- see: A pattern can follow two rules at once. Watch the colour, then watch the size. (picture: large red circle, small red circle, large blue circle, small blue circle, large red circle)
- watch: The colour goes red, red, blue, blue. The size goes big, small. Next is a small red circle. (picture: large red circle, small red circle, large blue circle, small blue circle, large red circle, small red circle)
- try: What comes next? (picture: large red circle, small red circle, large blue circle, small blue circle, large red circle, small red circle, blank)
  - then: The colour goes red, red, blue, blue. The size goes big, small. Next is a big blue circle.
- remember: Follow one rule at a time. (picture: large red circle, small red circle, large blue circle, small blue circle, large red circle)

### Lesson

- see: This picture is made of stripes. Each stripe is one colour. (picture: grid: yellow square | yellow square | yellow square | blue square | blue square | blue square | yellow square | yellow square | yellow square)
- watch: A piece is missing. Follow the lines and colours into the gap. The stripe is yellow, so the piece is yellow. (picture: grid: yellow square | yellow square | yellow square | blue square | blue square | blue square | yellow square | yellow square | blank)
- try: Which piece finishes the picture? (picture: grid: green square | orange square | green square | orange square | blank | orange square | green square | orange square | green square)
  - then: Follow the lines and colours into the gap.
- remember: The piece must match every edge. (picture: grid: yellow square | yellow square | yellow square | blue square | blue square | blue square | yellow square | yellow square | yellow square)

- Options: cow | **snow**
- Answer: snow
- Explanation, spoken: The snow is the one that is very cold.
- Explanation, shown: The snow is the one that is very cold.
- Explanation picture: snow

**Seed 2** (choice, look)

- On screen: Which one opens a lock?
- Options: umbrella | **key**
- Answer: key

- Explanation, shown: The key is the one that opens a lock.

- Answer: key

### Lesson

- see: A riddle gives clues. The clues point to one picture. (picture: clock and shoe)
- watch: Which one tells the time? The clock is the one that tells the time. (picture: clock)
- try: Which one can fly? (picture: shoe and bird)
  - then: The bird is the one that can fly.
- remember: Listen to every clue. (picture: clock and bird)
